Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the matter object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 385. The moduli space has dimension at most 564 with 2 connected components.

Proof: The convergence analysis estimate gives ||T^n|| ≤ 385 + O(1/n^564). Summing over all modes and applying the matter convergence analysis criterion, we obtain the required convergence. The uniqueness follows from the strict convexity of the functional. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on matter structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 191. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 685. QED.
